## 1. Stairs Races for 2 Players (`stairs-races-for-2-players`)

- **Slug**: `stairs-races-for-2-players`
- **Category**: Action
- **Word Count**: 123 words
- **Meta Title**: `Stairs Races for 2 Players Online | BlockHub.games` (50 chars)
- **Meta Description**: `Race 1v1 on one screen in Stairs Races for 2 Players. Gather colored blocks, construct steps, and sprint to the summit platform on BlockHub.games.` (146 chars)

### About Text

Stairs Races for 2 Players is a head-to-head 1v1 racing competition where two players compete on the same screen to build and ascend their staircase. Player 1 commands the blue stickman, while Player 2 takes charge of the red stickman. Throughout each stage, colored blocks matching each runner scatter across the floor. You must run across the arena, gather blocks of your designated color, and deliver them directly to your personal staircase to construct and paint the steps. The more blocks you retrieve and stack, the higher your runner climbs toward the elevated finish platform. Speed, route efficiency, and avoiding missteps decide the winner, as the first player to fully construct their staircase and step onto the summit platform claims the match victory.

### How to Play Text

Player one blue moves with WASD keys or the touch screen joystick on the left. Player two red moves with arrow keys or the touch screen joystick on the right.

### Controls Guide

| Input Key / Control | In-Game Action |
|---|---|
| `Player 1 (Blue)` | WASD Keys or Left Touch Joystick - Move & gather blocks |
| `Player 2 (Red)` | Arrow Keys or Right Touch Joystick - Move & gather blocks |

### Strategic Tips

- Gather large batches of your color blocks in a single pass to build multiple stairs at once.
- Watch your opponent's progress to decide whether to gather nearby blocks or sprint straight to your staircase.

### FAQ

**Q: How do you win a match in Stairs Races for 2 Players?**  
A: You win by collecting blocks of your runner's color, placing them on your staircase, and being the first to step onto the top platform.

**Q: Can two people play on the same keyboard?**  
A: Yes, Player 1 moves using WASD while Player 2 controls their stickman using the Arrow Keys.

## 2. Stickboy Hook (`stickboy-hook`)

- **Slug**: `stickboy-hook`
- **Category**: Action
- **Word Count**: 126 words
- **Meta Title**: `Stickboy Hook - Swing & Fly Online | BlockHub.games` (51 chars)
- **Meta Description**: `Grapple and swing through 200+ obstacle courses in Stickboy Hook on BlockHub.games. Master pendulum momentum and beat the clock with zero downloads.` (148 chars)

### About Text

Stickboy Hook is a one-tap physics swinging challenge centered on momentum, gravity, and aerial navigation. Players guide a stickman through dynamic courses filled with gaps, hazards, ramps, and suspended anchor points. Holding down your input casts a magical rope hook toward overhead anchors, pulling your character into a smooth pendulum arc. Releasing the hold flings the runner through open air, letting you catch subsequent grapple points in continuous sequence. Careful release timing is vital to avoid fatal spikes, slide down angled ramps, and rocket past the finish line. The game features three distinct play options: a Classic mode offering over 200 handcrafted levels of escalating difficulty, a timed Hook Mode dedicated to pure speed swinging against the clock, and competitive leaderboards for setting record completion times.

### How to Play Text

Tap and hold to throw the hook and swing on the rope. Release to let go and fly through the air. On desktop left mouse button spacebar. On mobile tap and hold anywhere on the screen.

### Controls Guide

| Input Key / Control | In-Game Action |
|---|---|
| `Mouse / Spacebar (PC)` | Click or press Spacebar to latch hook and swing; release to launch into the air |
| `Touch (Mobile)` | Tap and hold anywhere on the screen to grapple; release to fly |

### Strategic Tips

- Release your grapple at the peak of your forward swing arc to gain maximum launch distance.
- Look ahead for spike traps before letting go so you can hook the next anchor without dropping into hazards.

### FAQ

**Q: What game modes are included in Stickboy Hook?**  
A: Stickboy Hook features Classic mode with 200+ levels, a timed Hook Mode, and competitive leaderboards.

**Q: How do you control swings on mobile?**  
A: Tap and hold anywhere on the touchscreen to attach the rope hook, and lift your finger to release and fly.

Along the course, hostile forces deploy attack helicopters, armored machine gun jeeps, rocket launcher infantry, and giant battle tanks to halt your advance. ### About Text

Oil Tanker Simulator places you in command of a heavy commercial fuel transport truck tasked with hauling volatile liquid cargo across demanding highway networks and congested city routes. Safe delivery requires adapting to realistic commercial truck physics, where heavy payload inertia, extended stopping distances, and wide turning arcs influence every movement. Drivers must navigate unpredictable civilian traffic, execute wide turns around street corners, and negotiate changing road conditions while racing against delivery timers. Successful cargo drop-offs award in-game rewards that mark your progression as a veteran transport operator. The simulation supports multiple perspective cameras to aid tight dock maneuvering, vehicle resets for accidental rollovers, and responsive handbrake controls for emergency stops.

### How to Play Text

W / Up Arrow: Accelerate. S / Down Arrow: Brake / Reverse. A / Left Arrow: Steer Left. D / Right Arrow: Steer Right. Space: Handbrake. C: Change Camera View. R: Reset Vehicle. P: Pause Game.

| Input Key / Control | In-Game Action |
|---|---|
| `W / Up Arrow` | Accelerate engine throttle |
| `S / Down Arrow` | Apply foot brakes and reverse |
| `A / D or Left / Right` | Steer commercial tanker |
| `Spacebar` | Engage emergency handbrake |
| `C Key` | Toggle between camera view perspectives |
| `R Key` | Reset truck orientation after rollovers |
| `P Key` | Pause active delivery mission |

### Strategic Tips

- Begin braking well before approaching red intersections to counteract your trailer's forward momentum.
- Switch to external camera views using the C key to verify blind spots before backing into loading bays.

### FAQ

**Q: What routes do you drive in Oil Tanker Simulator?**  
A: Deliveries take you across busy city streets, open express highways, and winding transport routes.

**Q: What key changes the camera angle?**  
A: Press the C key on your keyboard to switch between interior and exterior camera angles.

### About Text

Dead Rails is an atmospheric Wild West post-apocalyptic survival game where an unknown viral outbreak has transformed frontier settlements into zombie-infested ruins. Your primary lifeline is a fortified armored train rolling across hazardous tracks toward a rumored cure at the final rail depot. Surviving the journey requires disembarking at abandoned frontier towns to explore decaying buildings, collect rare gear, gather vital resources, and scavenge fuel tanks to keep the locomotive running. Along each stretch of railroad, players must defend the locomotive from aggressive zombie raids, eliminate mutant creatures, and invest scavenged materials into weapon enhancements and train armor. The game blends direct on-foot tactical shooting with active locomotive speed management across unforgiving territory.

### How to Play Text

PC: W A S D - movement, E - pick up item, G - discard item, LKM - drag or shoot. Train control: W - forward, S - stop, Space - get off the train. Mobile device: Left joystick - movement, Right side - camera rotation and buttons.

### Strategic Tips

- Never leave your train parked in one spot for too long, as idling attracts aggressive mutant hordes.

### About Text

Angry Zombies War Game is a physics-based projectile puzzle game where players eliminate undead targets using slingshot ballistics. Pulling back on your skeletal skull projectile adjusts shot power and launch trajectory, while releasing it flings the heavy stone skull toward fortified zombie perches. Stages feature destructible wooden structures, stacked TNT crates, metal girders, and rolling environmental hazards designed to crush shielded zombies through chain reactions. The game provides two distinct difficulty settings: Easy mode, which displays a helpful predictive trajectory line for aligning angle shots, and Hard mode, which removes the aim sight entirely to test your instinctive spatial estimation. Clearing every undead creature using the fewest skull projectiles possible awards maximum level clearance.

### How to Play Text

To click any button and fling the skull, use the mouse. Click and drag back on the skull to aim and set power, then release to launch.

### Controls Guide

| Input Key / Control | In-Game Action |
|---|---|
| `Mouse Drag & Release` | Click and pull back on the skull to set power and trajectory; release to launch |

### Strategic Tips

- Aim for the base support beams of high wooden towers to trigger structural collapses that crush lower zombies.
- Use Easy mode to familiarize yourself with bounce angles before testing your skills in Hard mode without aim sights.

### FAQ

**Q: What is the difference between Easy and Hard mode?**  
A: Easy mode provides a visible trajectory sightline, while Hard mode removes the aim sight for an expert challenge.

**Q: How do you aim skulls in Angry Zombies War Game?**  
A: Click and hold the skull with your mouse, drag backward to adjust trajectory, and let go to shoot.

## 115. Wheelie Bike for 2 Players (`wheelie-bike-for-2-players`)

### About Text

Wheelie Bike for 2 Players is a physics balance duel where two players compete locally as the Purple and Green stickmen. Taking place over a strict 60-second round timer, the goal is straightforward: pull up your bike's handlebars and maintain a continuous rear-wheel wheelie for as long as possible without letting your front tire touch down or flipping backward onto your helmet. The player who racks up the highest accumulated wheelie duration before the clock runs out claims the match victory. Master subtle throttle taps, find your motorcycle's sweet balance angle, and out-balance your friend in head-to-head competition on a single device.

### How to Play Text

player 1 press W or touch screen for make wheelie player 2 press arrow up or touch screen for make wheelie

### Controls Guide

| Input Key / Control | In-Game Action |
|---|---|
| `Player 1: W Key or Touch Screen` | Apply throttle and raise front wheel to initiate wheelie |
| `Player 2: Up Arrow Key or Touch Screen` | Apply throttle and raise front wheel to initiate wheelie |

### Strategic Tips

- Tap the throttle in short bursts rather than holding it down continuously to avoid flipping over backward.
- If your front wheel starts dipping toward the pavement, apply a quick double-tap to pop it back up.
- Keep an eye on the 60-second timer; consistency across multiple short wheelies beats a single round-ending crash.

### FAQ

**Q: How is the winner determined in Wheelie Bike for 2 Players?**  
A: The player who accumulates the highest total wheelie time during the 60-second match wins.

**Q: What happens if your bike flips over?**  
A: Flipping backward crashes your stickman, resetting your bike to the track and costing valuable seconds.

### Facts Used

- Fast-paced local 2-player physics game competing as Purple and Green stickmen
- Objective: hold wheelie for as long as possible within 60-second time limit
- Winner is player with highest accumulated wheelie time; master physics to avoid flipping
- P1 press W or touch, P2 press Up Arrow or touch

However, road capacity is strictly limited: allowing the street to fill up with unassigned vehicles gridlocks the entire intersection and results in failure. Players must plan multiple moves ahead, temporarily staging cars to expose underlying colors and sorting full matching fleets into their designated garages without overflowing the roadway.

- Use shuffle boosters when all available garages are locked by mismatched lead vehicles.

### FAQ

**Q: How do you win a level in Car Sort?**  
A: Sort every vehicle into a garage where all parked cars share the exact same matching color.

Sokoban Master delivers the definitive crate-pushing warehouse logic experience inspired by the timeless puzzle genre. As a warehouse logistics operator navigating cramped brick storage rooms, your goal is pushing every wooden freight crate onto designated floor target markers. The fundamental rule of the warehouse never bends: crates can only be pushed forward, never pulled backward! A single careless shove into a corner or against a flat wall can render a crate permanently irretrievable. Progress through beginner-friendly warehouse layouts into diabolical spatial challenges, earn efficiency stars by minimizing total steps, undo accidental moves, and unleash your creativity with the built-in Level Editor.

Desktop Arrow Keys or WASD mdash Move Z mdash Undo move Mobile Touch click the game board to move Use the on-screen D-pad controls

| `Touch D-Pad / Tap Board` | Move worker and push crates on mobile touchscreens |

### Strategic Tips

- Never push a crate flush into a dead corner unless that specific corner tile is an active target depot.
- Work from the farthest target markers backward so previously parked crates do not obstruct your route.
- Use the Z key to undo mistakes immediately rather than restarting complex multi-crate stages from scratch.

### FAQ

**Q: Can you pull crates in Sokoban Master?**  
A: No, crates can only be pushed forward from behind; pulling is physically impossible in Sokoban.

## 141. Pixel Feast (`pixel-feast`)

### About Text

Pixel Feast is a visual logic puzzle where your goal is devouring beautiful pixel-art mosaics down to the bare canvas. In each stage, a hidden portrait—ranging from rockets and blooming flowers to exotic wildlife—is formed from hundreds of colored pixel cubes. Positioned above, a storage tank holds stacked color blocks. Tapping the lowest block in any tank column cracks it open, dropping miniature colored eaters into one of five available feeder slots. The eaters sprint across the canvas, munching every matching pixel until satisfied. However, with only five feeder slots, opening a color block that the canvas no longer requires clogs a slot permanently! Manage your order of operations across 134 handcrafted levels.

### How to Play Text

Tap or click a block on the bottom row of the tank That is the only control Only the lowest block in each column can be opened Mouse or touch portrait screen

### Controls Guide

| Input Key / Control | In-Game Action |
|---|---|
| `Left Mouse Button / Tap` | Tap the lowest block in a tank column to crack it open and release eaters |
| `Booster Icons` | Trigger hand pull, extra slot, or rainbow cannon boosters |

### Strategic Tips

- Inspect the picture carefully; never crack a color block if the canvas has no corresponding pixels remaining.
- Prioritize opening colors that free up underlying blocks needed for other sections of the picture.
- Deploy the rainbow cannon booster when multiple feeder slots are clogged with exhausted colors.

### FAQ

**Q: How many feeder slots do you get in Pixel Feast?**  
A: You have exactly five active feeder slots; if all five become occupied with unusable blocks, the level ends.

**Q: What do the three boosters do?**  
A: The hand pulls any block out, the plus icon grants an extra slot, and the rainbow cannon clears loaded blocks.

### Facts Used

- Pixel-art puzzle eating pictures clean built from colored pixels
- Tap lowest block on tank to release eaters into 5 slots; order is the whole game
- 134 hand-tuned levels with rockets, animals, food, flowers
- Boosters: hand pull, extra slot, rainbow cannon; only lowest block in column can be opened

Once drawn, realistic ball physics take over as the soccer ball rolls and ricochets along your line! Each stage introduces tricky environmental mechanisms, including gusting wind zones that blow shots off course, icy friction-free platforms, bouncy rebound walls, and moving laser gates. Deduce the winning route and restart anytime to refine your strategy.

Left click drag Hold down the left mouse button and drag the cursor to draw the line that will guide the ball toward the goal Clear Retry You can restart the level at any time using the reset button to redraw your strategy

### Strategic Tips

- Account for wind velocity by drawing your path curve slightly against the blowing wind direction.
- Draw guide rails that funnel the ball directly into the net rather than flat ramps that allow bounce-outs.
- Use the instant reset button freely whenever a drawn path misses the goal to experiment with new angles.

### FAQ

**Q: How does the soccer ball interact with drawn lines?**  
A: The ball follows and bounces along the physical surface line you sketch, subject to gravity and momentum.

### About Text

Soccer Drop Game merges soccer scoring with timing-focused physics drop puzzles. Suspended high above the goalmouth, a soccer ball swings or rests on movable platforms. Your objective is choosing the exact split-second to release the ball, allowing gravity, momentum, and obstacle physics to guide it safely into the net. Along the descent, the ball must bounce off angled wooden ramps, slide across icy chutes, avoid swinging hazard bars, and ricochet past moving blocker pads. Precise timing, spatial foresight, and quick reaction skills are required to solve every challenging drop puzzle across dozens of testing stages.

### How to Play Text

Mouse click or tap to play

| Input Key / Control | In-Game Action |
|---|---|

- Wait for moving obstacle platforms to align with your desired drop path before releasing the ball.
- Calculate the bounce angle off wooden ramps to ensure the ball doesn't deflect out of bounds.
- Observe obstacle cycle timing carefully; patience is essential for clearing late-game puzzle stages.

### FAQ

**Q: How do you play Soccer Drop Game?**  
A: Click or tap to release the suspended soccer ball at the right moment so it bounces cleanly into the goal.

**Q: What happens if the ball misses the goal?**  
A: If the ball falls out of bounds or hits a hazard, the stage immediately restarts for another try.

### About Text

Rolling Football is an inventive physics puzzle game where you construct the playing field to guide a rolling soccer ball into the net. Given an assortment of movable items—including angled wooden ramps, elevated bounce platforms, wind fans, and conveyor belts—you must position and orient each object across the stage. Once your blueprint is set, release the ball and watch gravity and physics do the work! If the ball hits an obstacle or rolls off course, adjust your ramp angles and try again. Each progressive level introduces fresh mechanical obstacles that challenge your logic, spatial reasoning, and timing.

### How to Play Text

Use mouse to play

### Controls Guide

| Input Key / Control | In-Game Action |
|---|---|

### Strategic Tips

- Position wind fans directly beneath platform gaps to lift the rolling ball across deep chasms.
- Angle wooden ramps gently so the ball maintains enough rolling momentum to reach the goal line.
- Test your path frequently with single-item adjustments to pinpoint exactly where the trajectory fails.

### FAQ

**Q: How do you score in Rolling Football?**  
A: Position ramps, fans, and platforms to construct a continuous path that guides the rolling ball into the net.

**Q: Can you reposition objects after placing them?**  
A: Yes, click and drag placed ramps and fans anytime to refine your layout until the ball reaches the goal.

### Facts Used
